Abstract: With the rapid evolution of 5G and emerging 6G networks, reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) have become a critical technology for enhancing wireless communication scenarios. However, optimizing RIS-assisted multi-user systems typically introduces high-dimensional physical layer variables and non-convex Pareto-optimal rate sets, posing severe computational challenges for real-time applications. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a dimension-reduced, hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL) framework, termed Pareto-aware autoencoder-assisted RL (PAAERL), to optimize online resource allocation in RIS-assisted Internet of Things (IoT) networks. Our approach first substitutes high-dimensional continuous RIS beamforming variables with lower-dimensional weight vectors that strictly represent the Pareto-optimal frontier, theoretically avoiding geometric information loss across both convex and non-convex rate regions. To further mitigate the curse of dimensionality in dense networks, an autoencoder architecture is integrated to execute a secondary, data-driven compression phase, mapping the priority space into a highly condensed continuous latent action space. Extensive simulations conducted across practical communication scenarios, including multi-user mobile edge computing (MEC) networks, demonstrate that the proposed PAAERL framework drastically reduces offline training times, accelerates online policy convergence, and significantly decreases overall network costs compared to state-of-the-art benchmarks, underscoring its exceptional scalability and practical viability for next-generation intelligent IoT environments.

</details>


### [279] [Radio-Optical Confluence in Intelligent Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13098)
*Akshita Gupta,Devika Dass,Agastya Raj,Carlos Natalino,Marco Ruffini,Paolo Monti,Daniel Kilper*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了无线接入网络（RAN）在面临密集化挑战时，探索更高效、可扩展架构的最新进展，重点关注从无线电与光收敛迈向全面融合的演进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无线接入网络密度的不断增加，传统架构面临容量、能效和可扩展性等方面的挑战，亟需探索新的架构方案以应对这些压力。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，梳理并分析近年来的研究成果，特别关注从无线电与光收敛到全面融合的技术演进。

Result: 总结了当前技术发展的主要趋势、关键挑战以及潜在解决方案，指出全面融合在提升网络效率与可扩展性方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 全面融合被认为是应对RAN密集化挑战的有前景方向，未来研究需进一步探索其实现路径与标准化进程。

Abstract: Challenges associated with densification of radio access networks are motivating exploration of more efficient and scalable architectures. We examine recent progress in one direction that involves moving beyond radio and optical convergence to full confluence.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [280] [RSMA-Enabled ISAC Networks with Fluid Antenna Systems: Stochastic Geometry Analysis and Low-Complexity Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12517)
*Abdelhamid Salem,Hana Shamata,Salma Elkawafi,Khaled M. Rabie,Xingwang Li,Turki Essa Alharbi,Mohammed S. Alzaidi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究多小区RSMA-enabled ISAC网络的下行性能，通过随机几何和拉普拉斯变换推导了闭合形式的解析表达式，提出了低复杂度资源分配框架，在显著降低计算复杂度的同时实现了通信性能的大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多小区ISAC网络中，如何在保证感知服务质量的前提下优化通信性能是一个关键挑战。传统迭代优化方法计算复杂度高，需要发展更高效的分析方法。本文旨在为RSMA-enabled ISAC网络提供高效的性能分析和资源优化方案。

Method: 采用独立泊松点过程建模基站、用户和感知目标的空域分布；利用FAS选择最佳天线端口；结合随机几何、次序统计和基于拉普拉斯变换的干扰分析推导闭合形式的解析表达式；通过表征公共预编码器的统计特性建立雷达SINR的近似表达式；提出低复杂度解析资源分配框架联合优化RSMA功率分配、通信感知波束折中和调度用户数。

Result: 推导了各态历经和速率的闭合形式解析表达式；建立了平均雷达SINR的可处理近似；提出的解析设计相比传统迭代优化方法显著降低计算复杂度同时实现几乎相同的通信性能；仿真验证了所推导解析表达式的准确性，并展示了在RSMA和速率及感知性能方面相比传统传输方案的显著改善。

Conclusion: 本文通过随机几何分析方法为多小区RSMA-enabled ISAC网络提供了高效准确的性能评估工具，开发的低复杂度资源分配框架在保证感知服务质量约束下实现了通信性能的最大化，为实际ISAC系统部署提供了理论指导和实践参考。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the downlink performance of multi-cell RSMA-enabled ISAC networks in which base stations (BSs), communication users, and sensing targets are spatially distributed according to independent Poisson point processes (PPPs). Each BS simultaneously serves multiple users using RSMA while exploiting the common stream as a dual-functional communication and sensing waveform. The users are equipped with FAS that selects the best antenna port to maximize the received signal quality. Closed-form analytical expressions are derived for the ergodic sum-rates by combining stochastic geometry, order statistics, and Laplace-transform-based interference analysis. Furthermore, a tractable approximation for the average radar SINR is developed by characterizing the statistical properties of the common precoder. Leveraging the derived analytical expressions, a low-complexity analytical resource allocation framework is proposed to jointly optimize the RSMA power allocation, the communication-sensing beam tradeoff, and the number of scheduled users while sat- isfying the sensing quality-of-service constraint. Compared with conventional iterative optimization approaches, the proposed analytical design significantly reduces computational complexity while achieving nearly identical communication performance. Simulation results verify the accuracy of the developed analytical expressions and demonstrate substantial improvements in both RSMA sum-rate and sensing performance over conventional transmission schemes.

</details>


### [281] [Feasibility and Convex Design of Probe-Position Matching in a Scanning X-Band Radar Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12602)
*William Khalili*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的微带天线阵列探针位置确定方法，通过在阵列优化前利用Floquet单胞分析建立电路模型，推导出阻抗匹配的解析条件，成功预测了X波段阵列的匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微带元件探针位置选择基于孤立元件的输入电阻，需要在阵列优化后才能确定。本文旨在提出一种在阵列优化前即可获得决策的方法。

Method: 将单一Floquet单胞解分解为馈入电感、携带探针位置的变压器变比和阵列负载谐振器，推导出三个解析结果：1）可达输入阻抗集合构成阻抗平面上的圆盘；2）精确匹配存在的充要条件；3）当条件不满足时最佳反射系数的计算方法。允许一个串联电抗作为第二变量时，最坏情况扫描扇区的反射系数呈拟凸性，可通过二分法全局求解。

Result: 应用于16元X波段阵列及对应的16×24格阵，孤立内部元件匹配至-16.94 dB，宽边工作反射为-6.6 dB。对于二维格阵，在每一个扫描角度下匹配判据均不满足，极小极大设计将最坏情况扇区反射从-2.6 dB改善至-11.1 dB。

Conclusion: 提取的电路模型成功预测了两种匹配设计的性能，且这些设计预测无需在全波模型中重新求解，证明了所提方法的实用性和准确性。

Abstract: The probe position of a microstrip element is normally chosen from the isolated-element input resistance. This paper replaces that procedure with a decision available before any array optimisation. A single Floquet unit-cell solution at one arbitrary probe position is decomposed into a feed inductance, a transformer ratio carrying the probe position, and an array-loaded resonator. Three closed-form results follow. The set of input impedances reachable by probe position and resonant length is a disk in the impedance plane. An exact match to a real reference Z0 exists if and only if Rp >= Z0 + Xp^2/Z0. When this fails, the best attainable reflection follows from the image of that disk under the bilinear map. Admitting one series reactance as a second variable, the worst-case reflection over a scan sector is shown to be quasiconvex and globally solvable by bisection. The results are applied to a sixteen-element X-band array and the corresponding 16x24 lattice. For the array considered, the isolated interior element is matched to -16.94 dB while the broadside active reflection is -6.6 dB. For the two-dimensional lattice the criterion is violated at every scan angle, and the minimax design improves the worst-case sector reflection from -2.6 to -11.1 dB. Both matching designs are predictions of the extracted circuit model and have not been re-solved in the full-wave model.

</details>


### [282] [Energy Efficiency Maximization for FAS-Assisted Downlink Communication in Mobile Embodied AI Networks (MEAN) over Interference Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12849)
*Ruopeng Xu,Zhaohui Yang,Jiaxiang Wang,Chenliang Wu,Zhaoyang Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了干扰信道下由流体天线系统(FAS)辅助的下行链路移动具身人工智能(MEAN)网络，其中多个基站-智能体对在频谱上复用。通过联合优化智能体位置、FAS端口选择和发射功率来最大化能效，并提出了一种迭代算法求解该混合整数非凸问题。仿真结果表明所提方案优于基准方案，并在强噪声条件下具有更好的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在干扰信道中研究FAS辅助的MEAN网络，多个BS-智能体对复用同一频谱。BS采用FAS改善通信质量，移动具身AI智能体根据环境感知信道信息(如信道干扰噪声比图CINM)调整位置。考虑同频干扰以及通信和智能体移动导致的能耗，目标是最大化能效。

Method: 建立能效最大化问题，联合优化智能体位置、FAS端口选择和发射功率。对于给定的智能体位置和FAS端口，推导出发射功率的闭式最优解。然后开发迭代算法，包括自适应FAS端口优化和顺序智能体位置优化，以及低复杂度功率更新方法。

Result: 仿真结果显示所提设计优于考虑的基准方案，并在强噪声条件下提供了改进的可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于FAS的MEAN网络能效优化框架有效，在干扰信道和能量约束下能够提升系统性能，特别是在恶劣噪声环境中表现优于现有方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a fluid antenna system (FAS)-assisted downlink mobile embodied AI network (MEAN) over interference channels, where multiple base station (BS)-agent pairs reuse the same spectrum. The BSs employ FASs to improve the communication quality, while the mobile embodied artificial intelligence (AI) agents can adjust their positions according to environment-aware channel information, such as a channel-to-interference-plus-noise map (CINM). Considering both co-channel interference and the energy consumption caused by communication and agent movement, we formulate an energy efficiency (EE) maximization problem by jointly optimizing the agent positions, FAS port selections, and transmit powers. To solve this mixed-integer non-convex problem, we first derive the optimal transmit power in closed form for given agent positions and FAS ports. We then develop an iterative algorithm with adaptive FAS-port optimization and sequential agent-position optimization, together with a low-complexity power-update method. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed design outperforms the considered benchmark schemes and provides improved feasibility under severe noise conditions.

</details>


### [283] [Antenna Positioning and Beamforming Optimization in MA Enabled Secure ISAC Systems: A Gradient-Based Meta Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12870)
*Zhendong Li,Yujie Zhao,Zhou Su,Xiao Tang,Zhiqing Wei,Ying Wang,Wen Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可移动天线(MA)的安全ISAC系统，通过联合优化天线定位、发射波束成形和人工噪声最大化系统保密速率。针对传统优化方法数学推导复杂和深度学习方法依赖训练数据的问题，设计了基于梯度的元学习(GML)算法，无需预训练即可实现高效优化。仿真结果验证了该算法在通信安全性和感知性能方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成感知与通信(ISAC)技术虽然显著提高了频谱效率，但在实际应用中面临严重的信息安全风险，即嵌入的通信信号容易被拦截。现有优化方法存在数学推导复杂、计算效率低的问题，而深度学习方案又过度依赖特定数据分布，泛化能力不足。因此，需要开发一种既能高效处理非凸优化问题、又具备良好泛化能力的新方法，以实现安全的ISAC系统设计。

Method: 首先，设计MA-enabled安全ISAC系统架构，利用可移动天线的空间自由度增强安全性。其次，建立联合优化问题，目标函数为最大化系统保密速率，优化变量包括天线定位、发射波束成形和人工噪声。针对问题非凸性和变量强耦合的挑战，提出基于梯度的元学习(GML)算法：为每个优化变量构建独立神经网络，以目标函数关于该变量的梯度为输入，网络输出为变量更新步长；通过约束处理和惩罚项构造全局损失函数，指导整个优化过程，无需预训练即可直接应用。

Result: 大量数值仿真结果表明，所提GML算法在通信安全性和感知性能两个关键指标上均达到令人满意的效果。与传统优化方法和现有深度学习方案相比，该算法无需预训练、数学推导简单，能够有效处理非凸优化问题中的变量耦合，展现出良好的收敛性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功解决了ISAC系统中的安全与效率平衡问题。提出的GML算法突破传统方法局限，通过梯度驱动的元学习机制实现了高效、无预训练的优化求解。该方案在保障通信安全的同时兼顾感知性能，为ISAC技术的实际部署提供了新的解决方案，对未来无线通信与感知融合系统的研究具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) significantly improves spectral efficiency but introduces security risks regarding the interception of embedded communication signals. This paper proposes an movable antenna (MA)-enabled secure ISAC system that utilizes the spatial degrees of freedom of MA to mitigate these risks. Then, a problem is formulated to maximize the system secrecy rate by jointly optimizing antenna positioning, transmit beamforming, and artificial noise. However, the principal challenge arises from the non-convexity of the optimization problem and the strong coupling of the optimization variables. Generally, traditional optimization methods for this problem suffer from complex mathematical derivations, while existing deep learning approaches rely heavily on the training data distribution. To address these issues, we introduce a gradient-based meta learning (GML) algorithm, which works without pre-training and demonstrates favorable performance. Specifically, the algorithm establishes a neural network for each optimization variable, where the gradient of the objective function with respect to the variable serves as the input, and the output of the network determines the variable's update step. By handling the constraints and constructing penalty terms, the global loss function is used to guide the optimization process. Extensive numerical simulations confirm that the proposed algorithm achieves satisfactory performance in terms of both communication security and sensing capabilities.

</details>


### [284] [Efficient and Accurate Surrogate-Assisted Electromagnetic Parameter Calibration for 6G Digital Twin Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12872)
*Xiaofan Zou,Pan Tang,Peijie Liu,Changyou Tai*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种代理辅助的灰狼优化器算法(SGWO-IM)，通过嵌入在线代理模型进行候选预筛选，结合自适应收敛和个体记忆策略，显著降低6G通信信道重建的计算开销（减少62.5%）并提高校准精度（RMSE从3.65dB降至2.97dB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真度光线追踪(RT)信道重建是构建6G无线通信数字孪生的基础步骤，但复杂的电磁材料参数精确校准面临两大挑战：巨大的计算开销和严格的精度要求。需要一种方法同时提升计算效率和校准准确性。

Method: 提出SGWO-IM算法：1）在评估流程中嵌入在线代理模型进行候选预筛选，减少对耗时的实际RT仿真的依赖；2）引入自适应收敛和个体记忆策略优化全局参数搜索路径，增强重建信道与测量数据之间的一致性。

Result: 在稠密城市场景中验证：算法仅需225次实际RT仿真调用（标准GWO需要600次），计算开销降低62.5%；RMSE从GWO的3.65dB降至2.97dB，显著提升精度。

Conclusion: SGWO-IM算法在效率和精度方面均取得显著进步，为电磁环境重建提供了有效平衡效率与准确性的解决方案，有助于6G数字孪生的构建。

Abstract: High-fidelity ray-tracing (RT) channel reconstruction is a fundamental step toward building digital twins for the era of 6G wireless communications. However, precise calibration of complex electromagnetic material parameters remains a dual challenge characterized by massive computational overhead and strict accuracy requirements. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose a Surrogate-assisted Grey Wolf Optimizer with Individual Memory (SGWO-IM) algorithm that simultaneously improves computational efficiency and calibration accuracy. In terms of computational efficiency, an online surrogate model is seamlessly embedded into the evaluation workflow for candidate pre-screening, substantially reducing the reliance on highly time-consuming real RT simulations. Regarding calibration accuracy, adaptive convergence and individual memory strategies are incorporated to optimize the global parameter search path, effectively enhancing the consistency between the reconstructed channel and measured data. Validated against measured channel data from a high-density urban scenario, the proposed algorithm requires only 225 real RT simulation calls compared to the 600 calls needed by the standard Grey Wolf Optimizer (GWO), cutting computational overhead by 62.5%. Concurrently, the final Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) is substantially reduced from the 3.65 dB of GWO to 2.97 dB. The results demonstrate that the SGWO-IM algorithm achieves significant advancements in both efficiency and precision, providing a solution that effectively balances efficiency and accuracy for electromagnetic environment reconstruction.

</details>


### [285] [GML-Based Optimization for Movable Antenna Wireless Networks: Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12882)
*Zhendong Li,Yujie Zhao,Zhou Su,Tom H. Luan,Zhiqing Wei,Ying Wang,Wen Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出梯度元学习(GML)优化框架，解决可移动天线(MA)网络中天线定位与波束赋形联合优化难题，通过数值仿真验证了框架性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可移动天线(MA)作为未来无线网络新兴技术，能通过额外空间自由度主动重塑无线传播环境提升网络性能。然而，充分发挥MA潜力需要联合优化天线定位与波束赋形，该问题非凸且高度耦合，现有解决方案存在显著局限性。

Method: 提出梯度元学习(GML)优化框架。首先阐述MA的硬件架构与信道特性，分析MA无线网络优化的主要挑战；随后介绍GML框架基本逻辑并与现有方法比较；讨论将该优化框架应用于MA网络的约束处理策略；通过具体案例数值仿真验证框架性能。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了所提GML框架在MA网络优化问题上的性能表现，展示了该框架相较于现有方法的改进效果。

Conclusion: 论文针对MA无线网络的联合优化挑战，提出GML优化框架并验证其有效性。同时概述了GML框架和MA无线网络的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Movable antenna (MA) is proposed as an emerging technology for future wireless networks. By leveraging the additional spatial degrees of freedom, MA can proactively reshape the wireless propagation environment, thereby enhancing network performance.However, fully unlocking the potential of MA networks necessitates the joint optimization of MA antenna positioning and beamforming. For this non-convex and highly coupled problem, existing solutions exhibit significant limitations. Therefore, this paper proposes a gradient-based meta learning (GML) optimization framework. Specifically, we first elaborate on the hardware architecture and channel characteristics of MA, based on which we analyze the primary challenges in optimizing MA wireless networks. Subsequently, we introduce the fundamental logic of the GML framework and compare it with existing methods. Furthermore, we discuss the constraint handling strategies for applying the proposed optimization framework to MA networks. A specific case is studied to show the performance of proposed framework based on numerical simulation. Finally, this paper outlines future research directions for both the GML framework and MA wireless networks.

</details>


### [286] [Input-Correlated Supervision Noise Limits the Benefits of OTA Training for Learned Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12918)
*Riku Luostari,Dani Korpi,Olav Tirkkonen,Harri Holma*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究发现，使用真实空口(OTA)测量数据训练学习型无线接收机存在根本性不对称：端到端接收机从全量测量数据中受益，而信道估计器在适中比例测量数据时性能最佳，全量测量反而导致性能下降，原因在于监督标签质量差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型无线接收机通常使用合成数据进行研究，但空口(OTA)测量数据对训练的影响尚不明确。这在实际部署学习接收机时是一个重要的实际问题。

Method: 在5.88 GHz频段开展测量实验，使用类似5G/6G的正交频分复用(OFDM)系统，在多样环境和移动条件下进行数据采集。训练两种神经网络：神经信道估计器和容量匹配的端到端神经接收机，使用测量数据与合成数据的混合数据集进行训练。

Result: 发现测量数据比例增加揭示出根本性不对称：测量数据持续改善端到端接收机性能，而信道估计器在中间比例时达到峰值，全量测量训练反而导致性能下降。实验表明这种差异源于监督目标：OTA信道标签来自有噪接收信号，包含与接收机输入相关的监督误差，而通过循环冗余校验(CRC)验证的解码比特提供无误差监督。

Conclusion: 结果为使用OTA数据训练学习接收机提供实用指导：端到端接收机受益于全量测量训练，而信道估计器受益于适中比例的OTA数据，但需要改善标签质量(如通过去噪)以获取进一步提升。

Abstract: While learned wireless receivers are typically studied using synthetic data, the impact of over-the-air (OTA) measurements for training remains unclear. We conducted a 5.88 GHz measurement campaign with a 5G/6G-like orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) system across diverse environments and mobility conditions, and trained a neural channel estimator and a capacity-matched end-to-end neural receiver using mixtures of measured and synthetic data. Increasing the OTA fraction revealed a fundamental asymmetry: measured data consistently improved the end-to-end receiver, whereas the channel estimator peaked at an intermediate fraction and degraded with fully measured training. We showed that this difference arises from the supervision target: OTA channel labels are derived from noisy received signals and therefore contain supervision errors correlated with the receiver input, whereas decoded bits validated by a cyclic redundancy check (CRC) provide effectively error-free supervision. A controlled denoising experiment confirmed that this correlation, rather than limited data diversity, caused the degradation. These results provide practical guidance for training learned receivers with OTA data: end-to-end receivers benefit from fully measured training, whereas channel estimators benefit from moderate OTA fractions but require improved label quality, e.g. via denoising, to unlock further gains.

</details>


### [287] [Wideband Large-Array Processing and Sparse Design for Angle Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13005)
*Ziyu Zhou,Wei Dai*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出宽带大阵列处理可通过稀疏物理阵列恢复大量角度像素，利用多频率虚拟阵列融合提高有效虚拟元素数量，并引入覆盖准则和确定性条件数界以实现稳定角度成像，仿真验证稀疏阵列可恢复远超物理天线数量的角度像素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阵列受限于物理天线数量，角度分辨率低；宽带信号不同频率产生不同虚拟阵列，融合后可大幅提升有效虚拟元素，从而以更少的物理天线实现高分辨率角度成像，但虚拟阵列不规则且不保证稳定恢复，需要一种高效准则和理论保障。

Method: 基于宽带虚拟阵列融合，定义覆盖准则估计稳定可恢复角度像素数，避免计算密集奇异值测试；推导满足准则的确定性条件数界，确保稳定角度成像；并设计非均匀稀疏阵列，最小化物理天线数量同时保持全视场恢复。

Result: 覆盖准则为系统稳定设计提供实用指导；所设计稀疏阵列可恢复比物理天线多十倍以上角度像素；理论条件数界验证了稳定恢复。

Conclusion: 宽带大阵列处理结合覆盖准则和稀疏阵列设计，实现了以极少物理天线恢复大量角度像素的稳定角度成像，显著扩展了阵列的感知能力。

Abstract: This paper shows that wideband large-array processing can recover a large number of angle pixels with far fewer antenna elements. The key advantage of wideband signaling is that different frequencies induce different virtual arrays, whose union forms a virtual array with a substantially increased number of effective virtual elements. Thus, a sparse physical array can support far more spatial samples than physical antennas. Motivated by this capability, we study the recovery of angular responses across the full field of view $[-90^\circ, 90^\circ)$, discretized according to the improved angular resolution, and refer to this sensing regime as angle imaging.
  However, the resulting virtual array is inherently irregular, clustered, and does not automatically guarantee stable recovery. To address this challenge, we introduce a coverage criterion that estimates the number of stably recoverable angle pixels, without computationally intensive singular-value-based conditioning tests over candidate image dimensions. For systems satisfying this criterion, we theoretically establish deterministic condition-number bounds that characterize stable angle imaging. Building on this criterion, we derive non-uniform sparse array designs that minimize the number of physical antennas while maintaining recovery over the full field of view. Simulation results show that the proposed criterion provides practical guidance for stable system design, and that the resulting sparse arrays can recover substantially more angle pixels than the number of physical antennas, with representative designs supporting over ten times as many angle pixels as physical antennas.

</details>


### [288] [Analysis and Approximation of a Spatially Wideband Antenna Array Factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13125)
*Marcin Wachowiak,André Bourdoux,Sofie Pollin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了均匀线性阵列的空间宽带（SWB）阵列因子，提出了基于卷积的通用近似方法，证明了带宽增加可有效抑制全填充阵列的旁瓣和稀疏阵列的栅瓣幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对均匀线性阵列的空间宽带阵列因子（SWB AF）进行分析，旨在理解宽带信号下阵列因子的行为特性，特别是不同空间窗和频谱窗对SWB AF的影响，以及带宽对旁瓣和栅瓣的抑制机理。

Method: 1. 首先针对窄带信号、大孔径且满足奈奎斯特准则的阵元间距，推导SWB AF近似表达式，考虑不同的空间窗和频谱窗；2. 进一步在均匀空间和频谱加权条件下，开发宽带信号的SWB AF近似方法；3. 最后提出通用SWB AF近似，将SWB AF视为空间变卷积，表示为空间窄带（SNB）AF与SWB核函数的卷积形式。

Result: 1. 对于全填充阵列，增加带宽可有效抑制阵列因子旁瓣；2. 提出的近似方法对多种带宽和阵元间距（包括稀疏阵列）均保持高精度；3. 对于稀疏阵列，带宽通过将其能量扩散到更宽的角度范围来抑制栅瓣幅度；4. 提供了栅瓣包络关于带宽-孔径乘积的近似表达式。

Conclusion: 本文建立了空间宽带阵列因子的通用近似框架，揭示了带宽和频谱加权对SWB AF的影响机制，为宽带天线阵列设计和分析提供了理论依据，尤其对稀疏阵列的栅瓣抑制具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: This work investigates the spatially wideband (SWB) antenna array factor (AF) of uniform linear arrays. First, the SWB AF approximation is derived for narrowband (NB) signals and a large aperture with element spacing satisfying the Nyquist criterion. The derivation accounts for different spatial and spectral windows. Next, an approximation of the SWB AF for a wideband (WB) signal is developed under uniform spatial and spectral weighting. The analysis shows that for fully populated arrays, increasing the bandwidth effectively suppresses the AF sidelobes. Finally, a universal SWB AF approximation is introduced, which is based on recognizing the SWB AF as a spatially variant convolution. In this formulation, the SWB AF is expressed as a convolution of the spatially narrowband (SNB) AF and the SWB kernel, providing insight into how the bandwidth and the spectral weighting affect the resulting SWB AF. The proposed approximation is shown to be accurate for a wide range of bandwidths and element spacings, including sparse arrays. In particular, for sparse arrays, the bandwidth enables suppression of grating-lobe amplitudes by spreading their energy over a wider angular range. An approximation of the grating lobe envelope as a function of the bandwidth-aperture product is provided.

</details>


### [289] [Exploiting Phase Noise for Sensing Privacy in ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13270)
*Musa Furkan Keskin,Kawon Han,Henk Wymeersch,Christos Masouros*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究分析了OFDM ISAC系统在本地振荡器(LO)相位噪声影响下的感知隐私问题。通过MCRB分析表明，Alice的LO质量恶化可通过LO不对称性显著扩大感知隐私优势（尤其对近距离目标），同时仅在噪声受限场景下对数据率产生适度降低，揭示了合法感知、窃听感知与通信三者之间的折中关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ISAC系统的发展，感知隐私保护成为关键挑战。本地振荡器不完美导致的相位噪声会影响系统的感知性能，而现有研究对相位噪声条件下感知隐私的定量分析尚不充分，尤其缺乏对合法节点与窃听节点LO不对称性的系统研究。

Method: 构建包含合法单静态ISAC收发器(Alice)、窃听双静态感知器(Eve)及通信用户(UE)的ISAC场景模型，所有节点均配备非理想LO。采用错误Cramér-Rao界(MCRB)分析方法，对Alice和Eve的单静态及双静态距离估计进行性能表征，其中差分相位噪声过程因共享LO呈现自相关(延迟相关)特性，因独立LO呈现互相关(延迟无关)特性。

Result: 仿真结果揭示了Alice合法单静态感知、Eve未授权双静态感知与UE通信三者间的折中关系，该关系受Alice的LO质量主导。通过Alice与Eve之间的LO不对称性，Alice的LO质量恶化可显著扩大对其有利的感知隐私差距（尤其针对近距离目标），同时在噪声受限场景下仅对数据率产生适度降低。

Conclusion: LO不对称性可作为增强ISAC系统感知隐私的有效手段。通过主动降低Alice的LO质量，可显著削弱Eve的感知能力，形成感知隐私优势，且对通信性能的负面影响可控。该发现为ISAC系统的隐私保护设计提供了新的理论依据和工程指导。

Abstract: We investigate sensing privacy in orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems under the impact of phase noise (PN) arising from local oscillator (LO) imperfections. Specifically, we consider an ISAC scenario comprising a legitimate monostatic ISAC transceiver (Alice), an eavesdropper performing unauthorized bistatic sensing (Eve) and a communication user (UE), each equipped with a non-ideal LO. To characterize sensing performance in the presence of PN, we carry out a misspecified Cramér-Rao bound (MCRB) analysis of monostatic and bistatic range estimation at Alice and Eve, whose differential PN processes are self-correlated (delay-dependent) and cross-correlated (delay-independent) due to the use of a shared and an independent LO, respectively. Simulation results reveal three-way trade-offs among legitimate monostatic sensing at Alice, unauthorized bistatic sensing at Eve and communication to the UE under PN, governed by the LO quality at Alice. Through the LO asymmetry between Alice and Eve, worsening LO quality at Alice can significantly enlarge sensing privacy gap in her favor, especially for nearby targets, with only a moderate reduction in data rate in noise-limited regimes.

</details>


### [290] [Deterministic Maximum Likelihood Direction Finding in the Mixture Noise of Gaussian and Spherically Invariant Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13294)
*Mingyan Gong*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对高斯与球不变（SI）混合噪声环境下的波达方向（DOA）估计问题，提出了ECME算法，解决了现有ECM算法收敛不良的缺陷，并推导了确定性Cramér-Rao下界（CRLB）。仿真表明，ECME算法收敛良好，且其DOA估计的均方根误差随信号功率增大而渐近逼近所推导的CRLB，验证了理论下界的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球不变随机过程能够有效建模脉冲噪声与不可靠测量，其混合高斯成分的确定性最大似然方向估计在近年来受到关注。然而，已采用的ECM（期望-条件最大化）算法在仿真中始终表现出收敛不良的问题，缺乏可靠的算法与理论性能界，因此需要改进算法并给出准确的性能评估基准。

Method: 本文设计并应用了ECME算法，该算法作为ECM的扩展，在每次迭代中额外利用实际对数似然函数先更新部分参数估计，且无需对所有参数进行初始化。同时，针对DOA估计器推导了确定性CRLB，并与仿真结果进行比较。

Result: 仿真结果显示：ECME算法能够实现良好收敛；随着信号功率增大，其DOA估计的均方根误差渐近逼近所推导的CRLB，表明所推导的CRLB是正确的理论性能下界。

Conclusion: ECME算法克服了传统ECM算法收敛不良的缺点，为混合噪声环境下的确定性最大似然波达方向估计提供了一种稳健的求解方法；所推导的确定性CRLB经过仿真验证，可作为该估计器的准确性能评估基准。

Abstract: Spherically invariant (SI) random processes can model impulsive noise and unreliable measurements. Recently, the mixture noise of Gaussian and SI components has been used in deterministic maximum likelihood direction finding. In this context, the Expectation-Conditional Maximization (ECM) algorithm, an extension of the expectation-maximization algorithm, has been applied and designed. However, simulation results show that the ECM algorithm always improperly converges. In this article, the ECM Either (ECME) algorithm, an extension of the ECM algorithm, is applied and designed, which additionally utilizes the actual log-likelihood function to first update partial parameter estimates at every iteration and does not need to initialize all parameter estimates. Moreover, the deterministic Cramer-Rao low bounds (CRLBs) of DOA estimators are derived and compared. Simulation results indicate that the ECME algorithm exhibits proper convergence and its root mean square errors of DOA estimates asymptotically approach the CRLBs as the signal powers increase, i.e., the derived CRLBs are correct.

</details>


### [291] [Foundation models for movement data: Are they ready for prime-time?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13316)
*Alexander Bräuer,Benjamin Cauchi,Nils Strodthoff*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 首次全面评估4个开源加速度计基础模型在19个健康监测任务中的性能，发现任务依赖性表现：监督模型在人类动作识别上与基础模型相当，特定基础模型在跌倒和压力检测中领先且对传感器位置变化更鲁棒；作为冻结特征提取器，基础模型在人口统计推断方面最强，睡眠分期性能对所有模型均接近随机水平。UniMTS提供最强表示，是唯一无需微调即可超越监督基线的基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大规模加速度计数据训练的基础模型已被提议作为健康监测的通用特征提取器，但缺乏关于其优势的系統性证据，需要全面评估其在不同任务中的实际表现。

Method: 对四个开源加速度计基础模型与监督基线进行系统比较，涵盖19个任务，包括日常生活活动、临床监测和生理推断三个领域。采用线性探测、冻结探测分析内部表示，进行概念发现分析，并提供基于场景的部署建议。

Result: 1. 任务依赖性能：人类动作识别中监督模型与基础模型表现相当；跌倒和压力检测中选定基础模型领先且对传感器位置变化更鲁棒；冻结特征提取器中基础模型对人口统计推断最强；睡眠分期性能所有模型均接近随机水平。2. 内部表示跨层相似性强，UniMTS提供最强表示且无需微调即可超越监督基线。3. 所有模型清晰捕捉高强度活动，但在久坐、复杂或模糊活动方面存在困难。

Conclusion: 基于评估结果提供场景化部署建议，识别出基于基础模型的活动概况推断作为超越固定类别分类的有前景研究方向，强调基础模型内部表示存在改进潜力，为健康监测领域的基础模型应用提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) trained on large-scale accelerometer data have been proposed as general-purpose feature extractors for health monitoring, but systematic evidence of their advantages is lacking. We present the first comprehensive evaluation of four open-source accelerometer FMs against supervised baselines covering 19 tasks across the domains of activity recognition including activities of daily living, clinical monitoring, and physiological inference. We find task-dependent performance results: supervised models remain competitive with FMs on human action recognition (HAR), with no consistent advantage for either, while selected FMs lead on fall and stress detection and are the most robust to sensor-placement variation. As frozen feature extractors, FMs are strongest for demographic inference, whereas sleep staging performance remains near chance level for all models. The internal FM representations show strong similarity across layers, highlighting potential for future FM improvements. Linear and frozen probing reveals that UniMTS provides the strongest representations and is the only FM that surpasses the supervised baselines without finetuning. Concept discovery analysis shows all models capture high-intensity activities clearly but struggle with sedentary, complex or ambiguous activities. We provide scenario-based deployment recommendations. Furthermore, we identify FM-derived activity profile inference-moving beyond fixed category classification-as a promising research direction.

</details>


### [292] [A Physically Consistent Assessment of Nearfield Beamfocusing into Occluded Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13350)
*Georg Schwan,Alexander Stutz-Tirri,Christoph Studer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文质疑辐射近场中复杂波束聚焦技术（如Airy波束）的必要性，发现简单视距(LoS)策略在部分遮挡区域接近最优，而完全遮挡区域可通过物理一致的电磁波传播模型直接获得闭式最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究探索了辐射近场的复杂波束聚焦技术，特别是Airy波束在障碍物后实现高效电磁波传输的潜力。作者质疑这种技术复杂度是否合理，旨在评估复杂波束聚焦技术的实际价值。

Method: 将遮挡区域分为部分遮挡和完全遮挡两类。对于部分遮挡区域，将简单的视距(LoS)策略（仅激活无遮挡视线的天线）与Airy波束进行性能对比。对于完全遮挡区域，建立能够捕获衍射等传播效应的物理一致电磁波传播模型，推导最优波束聚焦策略的闭式解析解。

Result: 部分遮挡区域：简单LoS策略接近最优，且复杂度远低于Airy波束，性能更优。完全遮挡区域：一旦获得合适的传播模型，最优波束聚焦策略具有闭式解，可直接计算。

Conclusion: 复杂技术（如Airy波束）在遮挡区域波束聚焦方面相对于简单替代方案的优势极为有限，其额外的复杂度并不必要。

Abstract: Recent work has explored elaborate beamfocusing techniques for the radiative nearfield, with some studies suggesting that certain beamshapes, such as Airy beams, can enable efficient electromagnetic (EM) wave transmission behind obstacles. In this letter, we ask whether the added complexity of such techniques is justified. We distinguish between partially and fully occluded regions. In the partially occluded region, where Airy beams are commonly employed, we show that a simple line-of-sight (LoS) strategy, which activates only antennas having an unobstructed view of the receiver, is near-optimal and outperforms Airy beams at substantially lower complexity. In the fully occluded region, we argue that accurate beamfocusing requires a physically consistent EM wave propagation model that captures propagation effects such as diffraction. Once such a model is available, however, the optimal beamfocusing strategy has a closed-form solution and can be computed directly. These results suggest that elaborate techniques, such as Airy beams, offer little benefit over simpler alternatives for beamfocusing into occluded regions.

</details>


### [293] [Heterogeneity-Aware Belief Synchronization for Semantic Communication in AI-Native 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13394)
*Muhammad Hannan Akram,Muhammad Abubakar Rashid,Wassi Haider Kabir,Haejoon Jung,Kapal Dev,Syed Ali Hassan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种异构感知信念同步框架，用于6G AI原生网络中多模态AI代理间的高效语义通信，通过MEC服务器上的潜空间翻译模型实现不同架构代理间的信念对齐，显著降低同步成本并减少知识漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络正在从通信基础设施演进为智能系统，其中大量自主AI代理分布在LEO卫星、HAPs、UAVs、边缘服务器和地面设备等平台上。语义通信需要通信代理具有充分对齐的信念才能正确解码消息，但在异构AI模型运行于多样化计算约束下并持续从本地环境获取不同知识的6G网络中，这一假设难以满足。

Method: 提出异构感知信念同步框架，使用部署在MEC服务器上的潜空间翻译模型。这些模型将一个代理的信念更新翻译为特定代理的知识，无需联合训练和同构模型架构。仅在必要时通过潜空间翻译模型交换紧凑的信念更新，从而保护隐私、降低同步成本并最小化本地知识漂移。

Result: 通过多层地面/非地面网络案例研究验证了该框架，结果显示其在异构代理中保持了低同步成本（以传输参数数量衡量）和低信念对齐误差。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了6G AI原生网络中异构代理间的信念同步问题，在不牺牲性能的前提下实现了高效的语义通信，为未来智能网络的发展提供了关键技术支撑。

Abstract: 6G networks will not be serving as communication infrastructures only; rather, they are expected to evolve into intelligent systems, where thousands of autonomous artificial intelligence (AI) agents are interconnected. The agents are deployed across a wide range of platforms including low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites, high-altitude platforms (HAPs), unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), edge servers, and terrestrial devices. These agents continuously observe their environment and exchange information. Semantic communication provides an efficient mechanism for exchanging meaningful information instead of raw data. However, its effectiveness depends on the communicating agents having sufficiently aligned beliefs to correctly interpret and decode the transmitted messages. This assumption becomes difficult to satisfy in the 6G network where heterogeneous AI models operate under diverse computational constraints and continuously acquire different knowledge from their local environments. This article presents a heterogeneity-aware belief synchronization framework for 6G AI-native networks. It uses latent translation models deployed on multi-access edge computing (MEC) servers. These models translate belief updates from one agent to agent-specific knowledge without requiring joint training and a homogeneous architecture of models. By exchanging compact belief updates through a latent translation model only when necessary, the framework preserves privacy, reduces synchronization cost, and minimizes local knowledge drift. We validate the framework through a case study on a multi-layered terrestrial/non-terrestrial network. Results demonstrate that it maintains low synchronization cost, measured by the number of parameters transmitted, and low belief alignment error across the heterogeneous agents in the case study.

</details>


### [294] [Low-Complexity Channel Parameter Estimation for Coprime HRIS-Assisted MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13436)
*Jean C. S. Ferreira,Melryllin G. O. Sousa,Fazal-E Asim*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于混合可重构智能表面(HRIS)辅助的上行MIMO系统的参数估计方案，通过稀疏互质阵列的主动传感元件与被动反射元件相结合，利用Khatri-Rao和Kronecker分解解耦级联信道，结合空间平滑和Root-MUSIC实现鲁棒的参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决全被动架构在硬件复杂度和估计精度方面的局限性，降低HRIS硬件复杂度，同时提高参数估计的准确性。

Method: 采用少量主动元件组成稀疏互质阵列进行本地传感，其余元件被动反射信号；利用Khatri-Rao和Kronecker分解高效解耦基站处的级联信道；通过空间平滑解决互质阵列秩缺陷问题，并利用Root-MUSIC实现鲁棒的角提取。

Result: 仿真表明该方法在不同场景下均具有高度鲁棒的估计性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的方案在降低硬件复杂度的同时，显著提高了HRIS辅助MIMO系统的参数估计精度，具有良好的场景适应性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a parameter estimation scheme for uplink MIMO systems assisted by a Hybrid Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface (HRIS). To reduce hardware complexity, the HRIS employs a small number of active elements arranged in a sparse coprime geometry for local sensing, while the remaining elements passively reflect signals. This simultaneous sensing and reflection significantly improves accuracy over fully passive architectures. The method leverages Khatri-Rao and Kronecker factorizations to efficiently decouple the cascaded channel at the base station. Furthermore, spatial smoothing resolves the coprime array rank deficiency, enabling robust angular extraction via Root-MUSIC. Simulations demonstrate highly resilient estimation performance across scenarios.

</details>


### [295] [On-Off Digital Noise Modulation with Fluid Antenna Systems over $κ$-$μ$ Fading Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13471)
*Daniel C. Araújo,André A. dos Anjos,Hugerles S. Silva,Constantinos Psomas,Robson D. Vieira*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出将开关数字噪声调制与流体天线系统结合，建立统一分析框架，评估其在AWGN和κ-μ衰落信道下的性能，推导出误比特率和分集阶数表达式，并通过仿真验证。结果表明，该方法在保证低复杂度的同时显著提升了可靠性，为物联网等场景提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在保持OODN调制固有低复杂度、非相干操作（无需载波相位恢复）的同时，利用流体天线系统的空间分集提升传输可靠性，从而支持能量高效的物联网和机器类通信系统。

Method: 提出了一种统一的分析框架，用于评估在AWGN和广义κ-μ衰落信道下，FAS辅助OODN接收机的性能。分析涵盖流体天线端口选择、可用端口数量及空间相关性，并推导出平均误比特率表达式及可达分集阶数，通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证了所有表达式。

Result: 推导出了平均误比特率和可达分集阶数的解析表达式，仿真验证了表达式的正确性。结果表明，FAS提供的空间分集显著增强了OODN传输的可靠性，同时保留了其低复杂度、非相干操作的特点。

Conclusion: 本研究通过整合开关数字噪声调制与流体天线系统，建立了面向能量高效物联网和机器类通信的新研究方向，显著提升了OODN传输的可靠性，同时保持了低复杂度、非相干操作的优势。

Abstract: This letter proposes the integration of on-off digital noise (OODN) modulation with fluid antenna systems (FASs). A unified analytical framework is developed to evaluate the performance of FAS- assisted OODN receivers over additive white Gaussian noise and generalized \k{appa}-μ fading channels. The analysis incorporates fluid antenna port selection, the number of available ports, and spatial correlation. Analytical expressions for the average bit error probability are derived and the achievable diversity order is characterized. All expressions are validated through Monte Carlo simulations. Results demonstrate that the spatial diversity provided by the FAS significantly enhances the reliability of OODN transmissions while preserving their inherent low-complexity, non-coherent operation without carrier-phase recovery. The proposed framework establishes a new research direction for energy-efficient Internet of Things (IoT) and machine-type communication systems.

</details>
